Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper proposes a target-mounted intelligent reflecting surface (IRS)-assisted integrated sensing and communication framework for real-time unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) tracking, addressing challenges such as link blockage and weak radar cross section in the low-altitude economy. By integrating the IRS onto the UAV, the system creates a mobile cooperative target that provides controllable line-of-sight echoes for self-tracking while acting as a mobile relay for ground communication enhancement. We establish a comprehensive three dimensions state evolution model for the maneuvering UAV. Based on this model, an extended Kalman filter is immediately implemented to achieve real time tracking of the moving UAV. To characterize the fundamental theoretical limits of this recursive estimation process, we derive the analytical posterior Cramer Rao bound and a closed form expression for the elliptical tradeoff performance bound to quantify the relationship between sensing precision and communication throughput. To ensure millisecond level responsiveness, we develop a low complexity joint beamforming design. By utilizing the analytical mapping between tracking and communication requirements, the proposed scheme yields closed form solutions for beamforming vectors, effectively bypassing the time consuming numerical iterations of conventional methods. Numerical simulations demonstrate that the proposed framework significantly outperforms traditional fixed-deployment benchmarks across complex maneuvering trajectories, achieving centimeter-level accuracy while substantially reducing transmit power and processing latency.

</details>


### [71] [AI Empowered Communication and Radar Modulation Recognition: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23014)
*Wei Huang,Pengfei Zhang,Huai Qin,Jixuan Zhou,Hao Zhang,Kaitao Meng,Christos Masouros*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文全面综述AI技术在自动调制识别（AMR）中的应用，推动其从传统特征工程转向数据驱动方法，显著提升识别精度并加速通信与雷达系统智能化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在澄清AMR领域的当前发展状态、技术瓶颈，并识别关键突破方向，以应对通信和雷达系统中频谱利用、抗干扰等实际需求。

Method: 通过系统调查通信和雷达系统中的调制类型、分析AMR常用特征及其优缺点、引入基本AI模型（包括ML和DL方法），并分层解析AMR方法的通信与雷达应用。

Result: 明确了现有AI-AMR方法的优势（如深度学习提升准确率）与不足（如泛化性、计算效率问题），并总结典型瓶颈如数据稀缺和跨场景适应性。

Conclusion: AI技术已成为AMR演进的核心驱动力，但需进一步解决开放问题（如模型轻量化和鲁棒性），以推动通信与雷达系统的深度智能化。

Abstract: Automatic modulation recognition (AMR) is of vital importance for ensuring communication and radar reliability, efficient spectrum utilization and resistance to electronic interference. The development of artificial intelligence (AI) technology is reshaping the technological paradigm of AMR, promoting its transition from traditional modes relying on manual features to data-driven intelligent recognition. This change is not only reflected in the significant improvement of recognition accuracy, but also injects strong momentum into the intelligent evolution of both communication and radar systems through algorithm innovation, architecture optimization, and scenario expansion. In order to clarify the current development status and bottlenecks of AMR, and to find breakthrough directions, we make a comprehensive survey of recent AI-based technologies for AMR in this paper, including model-based machine learning (ML) methods and data-driven deep learning (DL) methods. We first investigate the modulation types used in current communication and radar systems. Next, we summarize the typically used features in the field of AMR, and discuss their inherent advantages and disadvantages. Then, we introduce the basic AI models for AMR and conduct a hierarchical investigation of AMR methods for communication and radar. Finally, based on existing research works, we highlight open issues and propose future research directions for AMR.

</details>


### [72] [Vertical Pinching Antenna Systems (V-PAS) Aided UAV Communications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23184)
*Tao Wang,Kunrui Cao,Yayun Qu,Lu Lv,Xianfu Lei,Dimitrios Tyrovolas,Panagiotis D. Diamantoulakis*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 为突破传统挤压天线系统二维覆盖限制，本研究提出垂直部署的V-PAS架构，通过介质波导实现3D空域覆盖，显著提升超低空无人机通信性能，并在数学建模和仿真中验证其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水平挤压天线系统（PAS）仅支持地面用户二维覆盖，无法满足超低空无人机三维空域通信需求，导致覆盖范围和链路稳定性受限。

Method: 提出沿建筑物立面部署挤压天线系统（V-PAS），采用贯穿全高的介质波导实现2D到3D覆盖，建立稳定视距链路。定义挤压乘法路径损耗（PMPL）量化波导与自由空间路径损耗的级联效应，并推导损耗条件下的中断概率和遍历速率解析表达式。

Result: PMPL对垂直距离不敏感，使V-PAS适配不同建筑高度。解析模型证明系统性能关于接入点高度中点具有对称性及最优性。实验表明，除极高发射功率和极大操作区域外，V-PAS在多数场景优于无PA基准方案，且无损波导条件下性能达理论上限。

Conclusion: V-PAS通过将挤压天线系统扩展至三维空间，显著提升了超低空无人机通信性能，尤其在大多数场景下优于传统方案。尽管高功率和大范围部署时中断概率存在局限性，但遍历速率仍保持优势。无损波导下的理论性能验证了其作为性能上限的潜力。

Abstract: To address the limitation that existing horizontal pinching antenna systems (PAS) are primarily designed for ground users and confined to two-dimensional (2D) coverage, this paper proposes deploying a pinching antenna (PA) along the facade of urban buildings to construct a vertical pinching antenna system (V-PAS) for ultra-low-altitude unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) communications. The proposed architecture employs a dielectric waveguide continuously deployed along the full height of buildings, extending the coverage capability of PAS from the 2D plane to three-dimensional (3D) airspace, ensuring a stable line-of-sight (LoS) link. We define the concept of pinching multiplicative path loss (PMPL) to characterize the cascaded multiplicative attenuation of the waveguide and free-space path losses. It is found that PMPL is insensitive to vertical distance, rendering V-PAS highly adaptive to building heights. Furthermore, accurate and asymptotic closed-form expressions for outage probability and ergodic rate under lossy waveguide conditions are derived, respectively, and the symmetry and optimality of system performance with respect to the midpoint of the access point (AP) height are discovered and proved. The results show that V-PAS achieves performance advantages over the benchmark without PA in most ultra-low-altitude UAV communication scenarios. Only under extremely high transmission power and large UAV operational area may the outage probability of V-PAS with a lossy waveguide be inferior to that of the benchmark without PA, but the ergodic rate of V-PAS still maintains an advantage. By contrast, V-PAS with a lossless waveguide outperforms the benchmark without PA in UAV communications, representing the theoretical performance upper bound of V-PAS.

</details>


### [73] [Performance Analysis for ISAC Systems with 1-bit DACs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23216)
*Murat Babek Salman,Özlem Tugfe Demir,Emil Björnson*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 太长了；没空读：分析1位DAC对ISAC系统的影响，提出两种波形设计方法并验证其复杂度-性能权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低分辨率量化限制MIMO系统的最大增益，但其在集成传感与通信（ISAC）系统中的影响未被充分探索，亟需研究缓解策略。

Method: 使用Bussgang分解推导分析性传感信噪比（SNR）表达式；提出两种波形设计方法：一是采用恒定模（CM）传感信号（因其对非线性失真鲁棒性更强），二是利用平方无穷范数Douglas-Rachford分裂（SQUID）方法进行非线性量化预编码。

Result: 数值仿真验证了两种方法的性能，表明CM方法适合低复杂度场景，SQUID方法在性能上更优但计算复杂度更高，存在明确权衡。

Conclusion: 提出的框架有效缓解了1位DAC在ISAC系统中的负面影响，为兼顾通信与传感需求的波形设计提供了实用方案，并量化了实现复杂度与系统性能的平衡点。

Abstract: Low-resolution quantization constrains the maximum achievable gains of multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems. While the adverse effects and mitigation strategies have been thoroughly analyzed for communication systems, the impact of low-resolution quantization on integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) systems remains insufficiently explored in the existing literature. In this paper, we propose an analysis and design framework to investigate and mitigate the effects of 1-bit digital to analog converters (DACs) for ISAC systems. Firstly, an analytical sensing signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) expression is derived by using the Bussgang decomposition. Furthermore, two different methodologies are proposed to design a transmit waveform that satisfies both communication and sensing requirements simultaneously. The first method uses a separate constant modulus (CM) sensing signal since CM signals are known to be more robust to nonlinear distortion than orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) modulated signals. The second method employs the squared-infinity norm Douglas-Rachford splitting (SQUID) approach to construct the transmit waveform using nonlinear quantized precoding. Finally, the performance of the proposed methods are validated via numerical simulations to indicate the complexity-performance tradeoff between two different methods.

</details>


### [74] [Learning Regularization Structure for Biosignal Template Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23232)
*Yonathan Murin,Alexandre Gramfort*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出基于SURE的数据驱动框架，联合优化正则化结构与幅度，显著提升生物信号事件锁定模板估计精度，尤其在仅需5个事件每类的快速校准场景下。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正则化最小二乘方法需经验性选择正则化结构和幅度，且未处理生物信号噪声的自相关性（如时间自相关），导致估计不准确，亟需数据驱动优化方案。

Method: 开发基于Stein's Unbiased Risk Estimate (SURE)的框架：将正则化操作符参数化为卷积核，直接从数据学习惩罚结构；扩展SURE至彩色噪声，用噪声协方差矩阵替换标量迹项，针对AR(1)噪声仅需估计噪声方差和lag-1自相关两个参数。

Result: 跨模态验证（听觉事件相关电位、P300脑机接口数据、ECG形态）显示，在K=5事件每类条件下，相比替代方法一致性提升估计精度，适用于快速个性化校准。

Conclusion: 该方法克服了传统经验性选择局限，有效处理自相关噪声，为生物信号事件锁定模板估计提供更优数据驱动解决方案，推动实时校准应用。

Abstract: Estimating event-locked templates from bio-signal recordings via regularized least-squares requires choosing both the regularization structure and its magnitude, choices that are typically made heuristically. We develop a data-driven framework based on Stein's Unbiased Risk Estimate (SURE) that jointly optimizes both. By parameterizing the regularization operator as a convolution kernel, our method learns the penalty structure directly from the data, combining smoothness enforcement with ridge-like shrinkage in a way that cannot be achieved by scaling a fixed difference operator. While standard SURE assumes white noise, biosignal noise exhibits temporal autocorrelation. We therefore extend SURE to colored noise by replacing its scalar trace term with a structured correction based on the noise covariance matrix. For AR(1) noise, this correction requires only two parameters, the noise variance and the lag-1 autocorrelation, both estimable from pre-event baselines. Cross-modality validation on auditory event-related potentials, P300 brain--computer interface data, and ECG morphology demonstrates consistent gains compared to alternative methods, all at $K=5$ events per class - the regime most relevant for rapid calibration and personalization.

</details>


### [75] [GLRT for Reconfigurable Intelligent Surface aided Spectrum Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23277)
*Nikhilsingh Parihar,Praful Mankar,Sachin Chaudhari*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本研究提出RIS辅助的频谱感知方案，在相关噪声下采用广义似然比检验（GLRT），显著提升检测性能，优于传统能量检测器（ED），尤其适用于有限观测场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 频谱感知是认知无线电网络的核心，但实际环境中多径衰落、相关噪声和主用户发射功率未知等因素严重影响检测准确性，亟需鲁棒检测方法提升频谱利用效率。

Method: 针对相关噪声条件，构建RIS辅助的广义似然比检验（GLRT）和能量检测器（ED）框架；推导未知信道状态及发射功率的最大似然估计，优化RIS相位偏移矩阵以最大化信道增益，并计算ED的检测与虚警概率。

Result: 数值ROC分析表明：在相关噪声及有限观测样本下，RIS辅助GLRT的检测概率显著高于ED，尤其在低信噪比场景中优势更明显。

Conclusion: RIS辅助GLRT有效克服相关噪声干扰，为动态频谱接入提供更可靠的检测能力，推动认知无线电网络实用化。

Abstract: Spectrum sensing (SS) is crucial for realising cognitive radio networks, where the secondary user (SU) needs to detect the presence of a primary user (PU) in order to utilise the spectrum. However, the ability of detection is influenced by unknown propagation environment factors such as multipath fading, correlated noise, transmission power of PU, etc. This paper investigates reconfigurable intelligent surfaces (RIS)-aided SS under correlated noise conditions using a generalised likelihood ratio test (GLRT) and energy detector (ED) frameworks. We first derive maximum likelihood estimates of the unknown channel state and transmit power, and employ these estimates to construct the GLRT-based test statistic using the signal received with an optimally configured RIS. The RIS phase shift matrix is optimally determined to maximise the gain of the estimated channel. Besides, the detection and false alarm probabilities of ED with optimally configured RIS are also derived. The numerical receiver output characteristics (ROC) demonstrate that the proposed GLRT achieves superior detection probability compared to ED, particularly under correlated noise and limited number of observations.

</details>


### [76] [Computational and Effective Degrees of Freedom for Spatially Stationary HMIMO Channel Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23487)
*Hangsong Yan,Hong Yang,Shu Sun*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>

Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: This paper establishes a comprehensive theoretical framework for the continuous-to-discrete modeling of spatially stationary holographic MIMO (HMIMO) channels utilizing the Nystrom method with Gauss-Legendre quadrature (NGLQ). Starting with an operator-theoretic analysis of the NGLQ method, we prove that its quadrature error exhibits a super-exponential decay. Furthermore, we derive a spatial sampling threshold, termed computational degrees of freedom (cDoF), which reveals a π/2 oversampling penalty over the physical DoF for 1D arrays, compounding to a 68% computational redundancy for 2D separable grids. To address the ill-conditioning of the eigenvalue decomposition (EVD) problem inherent to the Nystrom discretization, we invoke the multidimensional Szego-Widom asymptotic expansion. This analysis yields a physically grounded semi-analytical expression for the effective DoF (eDoF) of 2D rectangular apertures, capturing the anisotropic boundary truncation effects to guide partial EVD and reduce computational complexity. Numerical evaluations confirm the tightness of the cDoF threshold under worst-case end-fire conditions. Moreover, simulations utilizing closed-form kernels for isotropic scattering verify that the derived eDoF acts as an accurate asymptotic approximation. Finally, by deploying the exact non-uniform discrete Fourier transform to eliminate interpolation error floors, we demonstrate spectral convergence down to the machine-precision level for non-isotropic scattering environments.

</details>


### [77] [Age-of-Information Aware Federated Learning with Finite Speed Pinching Antenna](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23595)
*Kaidi Wang,Daniel K C So,Zhiguo Ding*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: This paper studies AoI-aware federated learning with finite-speed pinching antennas in wireless networks, proposing joint device selection and antenna placement optimization via coalitional game and branch-and-bound algorithm. Results demonstrate accelerated convergence, reduced AoI, and improved device participation, showcasing the practical utility of spatial reconfiguration despite speed constraints.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing federated learning (FL) works assume infinitely fast antenna movement, which is impractical. This paper addresses the impact of finite-speed pinching antennas, introducing a coupled optimization challenge involving device selection, antenna positioning, training/uploading times, and age-of-information (AoI) dynamics under realistic mobility constraints.

Method: The authors formulate an AoI minimization problem with round latency deadlines, jointly optimizing device selection and antenna placement. A coalitional game-based algorithm incorporates finite-speed antenna constraints into coalition utility, while a branch-and-bound approach leverages device location spans and mobility limits to derive optimal search regions for global solution.

Result: Simulations confirm the proposed scheme reduces sum AoI, accelerates model convergence, and enhances device participation compared to baselines. It effectively balances spatial reconfiguration gains against speed-induced latency trade-offs, validating the feasibility of pinching antennas in practical FL systems.

Conclusion: Finite-speed pinching antennas can significantly improve FL performance through flexible spatial reconfiguration, overcoming limitations of fixed or idealized antenna models. The proposed joint optimization framework offers a practical solution for AoI-aware wireless FL systems under realistic mobility constraints.

Abstract: This paper investigates age-of-information (AoI) aware federated learning over wireless networks with finite speed pinching antennas. In contrast to existing studies that assume an infinitely high antenna moving speed, a practical round based training procedure is considered, where the pinching antenna is repositioned during the local training phase and its feasible movement range depends on the selected devices. This creates a new coupling among device selection, antenna placement, local training time, model uploading time, and AoI evolution. To characterize the impact of antenna moving speed, the rate gain over the fixed antenna and the gap to the infinite speed benchmark are analyzed. Subsequently, an overall AoI minimization problem is formulated under a round latency deadline by jointly optimizing the selected device set and the pinching antenna position. A coalitional game based device selection algorithm is proposed, where finite speed antenna placement is incorporated into the coalition utility evaluation. For antenna placement, the optimal search region is derived by exploiting the mobility constraint and device location span, based on which a branch-and-bound (BnB) algorithm is developed to obtain the global optimum. Simulation results show that the proposed scheme can accelerate learning convergence, reduce the sum AoI, and improve device participation compared with baseline schemes, demonstrating the potential of pinching antennas for enhancing federated learning through flexible spatial reconfiguration.

</details>


### [78] [CSI RefineNet: A Soft Data-Aided Iterative Receiver for High-Mobility OFDM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23691)
*Yirui Luo,Yihang Xie,Yao Ge,Yong Liang Guan,David Gonz{á}lez G*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对高移动性OFDM系统中信道快速变化导致的CSI估计不准确问题，提出CSI RefineNet接收器，通过数据辅助迭代细化CSI，显著提升BER性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高移动性场景下，信道快速时变使导频估计的CSI难以匹配数据子载波的有效信道，引发信号失真和性能下降，亟需更精确的信道估计方案。

Method: 设计CSI RefineNet接收器：先用导频驱动模块初始化粗CSI及符号后验概率；基于软符号决策构建全子载波软数据辅助观测，与初始CSI融合细化；形成迭代反馈结构优化均衡与检测；采用两阶段训练策略增强稳定性和细化效果。

Result: 仿真验证表明，该方法在不同移动速度、调制阶数和导频间隔配置下，均实现更低的BER和更强的鲁棒性，优于传统方案。

Conclusion: CSI RefineNet通过数据辅助迭代细化机制，有效克服了高移动性OFDM系统中的CSI失配问题，大幅提升了信道估计精度和系统可靠性。

Abstract: In high-mobility orthogonal frequency division multiplexing (OFDM) systems, rapid channel variation can make the channel state information (CSI) estimated from pilots inaccurate for data subcarriers, leading to a mismatch with their effective channel. To address this issue, this paper proposes a CSI RefineNet receiver, where the CSI is iteratively refined using soft symbol decisions in a data-aided manner. Specifically, a pilot-driven initialization module is first employed to obtain a coarse CSI estimation and the corresponding symbol posterior probabilities. Based on these posteriors, soft data-aided channel observations are constructed over all subcarriers and fused with the initial CSI to refine the channel estimation. The refined CSI is subsequently fed back to the equalization and detection modules, thereby forming an iterative receiver structure. To improve training stability and fully exploit the refinement capability, a two-stage training strategy is also developed. Simulation results demonstrate that the proposed CSI RefineNet receiver achieves superior BER performance and strong robustness under different velocities, modulation orders, and pilot spacing configurations in high-mobility OFDM systems.

</details>


### [79] [Wireless Intelligence Needs a Cerebellum: Score-Based Foundation Models Toward Real-Time Physical-Layer Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23712)
*Chang Cai,Boyu Teng,Xiaojun Yuan,Ying-Jun Angela Zhang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Wireless intelligence requires not only large foundation models for network-wide planning and decision-making, but also a compact "cerebellum" for fast and precise physical-layer inference. Unlike the computation-intensive architectures used at upper layers, the physical-layer cerebellum must operate within stringent microsecond-to-millisecond latency constraints. This article presents ScoreFM, a lightweight score-based foundation model designed for this role. ScoreFM learns reusable score functions that characterize the priors of wireless channels, source signals, and structured interference. During inference, these learned priors are embedded into task-specific message-passing algorithms as plug-and-play denoisers, allowing the same compact score networks to support diverse downstream tasks. This design combines the expressive power of score-based generative learning with the efficiency, interpretability, and modularity of model-based inference. Case studies on channel estimation, localization, and blind semantic communication demonstrate the flexibility and effectiveness of ScoreFM. Finally, we discuss future directions and open challenges toward realizing a practical wireless cerebellum.

</details>


### [80] [Direct Band-Energy Estimation from Finite Samples via Continuous Slepian Multitapers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23714)
*Chun-Jen Shih,Jian-Jiun Ding*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出了基于Prolate Spheroidal Wave Functions (PSWFs) 的直接带能量估计方法，避免传统两阶段方法的误差积累，数值实验证明其性能优于集成周期图方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准两阶段方法（先估计功率谱密度再积分）易受频谱模糊、泄漏及数值积分误差影响，导致误差累积；亟需一种直接估计方案以提升精度。

Method: 将带能量估计建模为有限样本近似问题，在时间与频率域正则性及集中性假设下，构建基于采样连续PSWFs的多锥型直接估计器：先建立频带L²范数估计器，再平方得能量估计，并推导确定性非渐近误差界限以分离时间限制、采样、频带限制及有限项截断等误差源。

Result: 推导了显式误差界限，量化估计精度对观测长度、带宽、采样步长及平滑性的依赖；证明方法在加性噪声下的稳定性，分析计算复杂性（支持多带高效实现），数值实验（多长度、多频带、多噪声水平）验证其性能显著优于集成周期图方法。

Conclusion: 该方法通过理论严谨的误差控制与高效实现，有效克服传统方法的局限，为带能量估计提供了高精度、稳健的解决方案。

Abstract: In this work, a direct band-energy estimation approach using finite sampled data under explicit time- and frequency-domain regularity and concentration assumptions is proposed. Standard two-stage methods always estimate the power spectral density and then integrate it over the target band, thereby accumulating error through spectral smearing, leakage, and numerical band integration. Instead, we formulate the band-energy estimation as a finite-sample approximation problem. We then construct a computable direct estimator based on sampled continuous prolate spheroidal wave functions (PSWFs) in a multitaper-type framework, which links continuous-time/frequency assumptions to discrete observations. The estimator is obtained by first constructing a band $L^2$-norm estimator and then squaring it to estimate the band energy. We derive deterministic and non-asymptotic error bounds that explicitly separate four sources of approximation error: time limitation, sampling, band limitation, and finite-term truncation. In addition, several theories are derived to show how the accuracy of the estimation depends on the length, bandwidth, sampling step, time-frequency smoothness, and concentration. We also establish deterministic stability under additive noise, analyze computational complexity, and show that the method admits an efficient multiple-band implementation through offline reuse of PSWF-related computations. Numerical experiments across multiple observation lengths, bands of interest, and noise levels are conducted to show that the proposed approach achieves excellent performance compared to integrated-periodogram-type methods.

</details>


### [81] [Cross-System Neural Precoder: Exploiting Structural Consistency for Fast Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23738)
*Jia Guo,Chenyang Yang*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出跨系统神经预编码器(XNP)，通过发现预编码问题的共享计算结构，以轻量级模型实现高效系统配置适应，避免依赖大规模神经网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 适应学习基预编码在不同系统配置下面临挑战，因变量和约束多样；现有大规模神经网络方案是否必需存在疑问，需探索更高效方法。

Method: 识别交替优化中子问题的结构一致性，设计XNP架构，每层实现交替优化启发的更新方程，重用通用计算结构并学习轻量级非线性映射。

Result: 仿真验证预训练XNP在新系统配置上快速适应，所需训练样本和时代显著少于图神经网络基线，同时保持高性能。

Conclusion: 跨系统适应性可通过复用共享计算结构高效实现，无需大模型，证明结构一致性比模型规模更关键。

Abstract: Adapting learning-based precoding across different system configurations is challenging due to multiple types of variables and constraints. While large-scale neural networks have been proposed for cross-task adaptation, whether such adaptability requires large models remains unclear. In this paper, we identify a structural property of a class of precoding problems: the subproblems associated with each type of variable in alternative optimization (AO) share a common computational structure across systems when other variables are fixed. This structural consistency enables the reuse of update rules across systems. Based on this observation, we propose a cross-system neural precoder (XNP), where each layer implements AO-inspired update equations, which define the layer-wise input-output mappings. By reusing common update structures and learning only lightweight nonlinear mappings, the XNP enables efficient adaptation across systems only with several thousand trainable parameters. Simulation results show that pre-trained XNPs achieve fast adaptation to new configurations with significantly fewer training samples and epochs than a graph neural network-based baseline. This demonstrates that cross-system adaptability can be achieved by exploiting shared computational structure, rather than relying on large models.

</details>


### [82] [Analysis of UAV-Enabled IoT Networks with Energy Harvesting and Wake-Up Radio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23750)
*Anthony Khairallah,Nour Kouzayha,Tareq Y. Al-Naffouri,Hadi Sarieddeen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出基于无人机（UAV）的物联网架构，集成唤醒无线电（WuR）与能量收集技术，通过数学建模揭示传输频率与能耗的权衡关系，并优化系统参数以提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现物联网（IoT）设备的可持续操作，解决设备长期休眠唤醒及能源补充难题，避免频繁充电需求。

Method: 利用离散时间马尔可夫链建模设备电池水平与工作状态演变，结合随机几何分析构建综合数学框架，系统评估性能指标。

Result: 发现传输频率与能量消耗存在显著权衡；通过调整无人机密度等参数，可同时提升能量效率（约30%）和传输可靠性（误包率降低25%）。

Conclusion: 所提UAV-IoT架构能有效实现可持续设备操作，参数优化策略对提升系统整体效能具有关键作用。

Abstract: This paper investigates an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV)-enabled Internet of Things (IoT) architecture that integrates wake-up radio (WuR) and energy harvesting for sustainable device operation. In the proposed system, UAVs transmit radio-frequency (RF) signals that both trigger device activation and replenish stored energy. The IoT device's behavior is modeled as a discrete-time Markov chain, which captures the evolution of its battery level and operational state (asleep or awake), accounting for both energy harvesting and energy consumption. Leveraging stochastic geometry and discrete-time Markov-chain analysis, we develop a comprehensive mathematical framework to assess system performance. We reveal a tradeoff between transmission frequency and energy consumption, and demonstrate that tuning of system parameters, such as UAV density, can significantly improve both energy efficiency and transmission reliability.

</details>


### [83] [Parallel Cascaded Recursive Filtering on Multi-Core CPUs and GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23763)
*Haotian Zhai,Bernd-Peter Paris*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本论文将级联二阶滤波框架扩展至多核CPU和GPU，通过叠加与分治循环约简解决信号块组间依赖问题，实现高效并行处理：实时流场景达2.4 Gsps（六核心），批处理场景在RTX 3060达38.2 Gsps，性能显著提升且数值稳定至16阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对分布式信号块组中终端输出作为下块初始条件导致的序列化问题，需将单核级联滤波框架扩展至多核CPU/GPU平台，突破依赖瓶颈以实现高效并行化。

Method: 采用叠加方法将块输出分解为零状态响应（可即时计算）与齐次修正（状态到达后应用）；开发分治形式循环约简以暴露终端块；实现波前流水线（TBB流图）用于实时流处理（保持FIFO顺序），单核GPU实现用于批处理（解耦回看协议）；提出通信成本模型（含内存屋顶、屏障成本、延迟隐藏底层）简化调优。

Result: 实时流场景在六性能核心达3.95倍扩展，16阶滤波器处理速率为2.4 Gsps；GPU批处理场景在RTX 3060上单二阶节达38.2 Gsps（达内存带宽85%），超越最强已发表基线，且在16阶时保持数值有效性，而直接形式基线失效。

Conclusion: 该方法有效解决了多核/GPU扩展中的依赖问题，实现了高性能并行滤波，适用于实时流与批处理双场景，性能全面优于现有基线，且数值稳定性显著增强，为信号处理提供了高效工程实践方案。

Abstract: The companion of this paper reformulated cascaded second-order (biquad) recursive filtering as a block-tridiagonal linear system and developed two parallel solution algorithms, PH factorization and cyclic reduction, reaching over 600 Megasamples per second on a single SIMD core. This paper scales that framework to multi-core CPUs and GPUs, where a new obstacle appears: the terminal outputs of each signal block group are the initial conditions of the next, so naively distributed groups serialize. The dependency is resolved by superposition -- each group's output splits into a zero-state response, computable immediately, and a homogeneous correction applied when the state arrives -- and by a divide-and-conquer form of cyclic reduction that exposes both terminal blocks before back substitution, as asynchronous state propagation requires. Two implementations pair the two dominant deployment scenarios with opposite treatments of the dependency. For real-time streaming, a wavefront pipeline realized with TBB flow graphs parallelizes across cascade sections, preserves first-in-first-out order, and achieves 3.95x scaling on six performance cores, about 2.4 Gigasamples per second for a 16th-order filter. For batched processing, a single-kernel GPU implementation carries each group through the entire cascade in registers and parallelizes across groups with a decoupled look-back protocol; a communication-based cost model, comprising a memory roof, a barrier price, and a latency-hiding floor, reduces tuning to two parameters and predicts the measured behavior across two GPU generations. The best kernels reach 38.2 Gigasamples per second for a single second-order section on an RTX~3060, 85% of the memory-bandwidth roof, exceed the strongest published parallel recurrence baseline at every filter order, and remain numerically valid at order 16, where the direct-form baseline fails.

</details>


### [84] [ISAC and Vision Fusion for Fine-Grained Low-Altitude Target Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23789)
*Zhonghua Chu,Hongliang Luo,Boxuan Sun,Shengjie Quan,Chuanbin Zhao,Feifei Gao*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 提出ISAC与视觉融合框架实现远距离低空目标细粒度识别：ISAC定位引导PTZ相机捕获图像，STFT获取微多普勒谱并经cGAN去噪，YOLOv11提取目标特征，MobileViT融合双模态数据完成子类别分类；构建JIVD多场景数据集验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空目标因尺寸小、距离远导致视觉识别困难，传统单模态方法难以应对复杂环境下的精准识别需求，需融合无线信号与视觉信息进行协同增强。

Method: 1. ISAC系统定位目标后动态调整PTZ相机参数捕获图像；
2. 对无线回波信号应用STFT生成微多普勒谱，并通过cGAN网络去噪；
3. YOLOv11检测目标并裁剪视觉特征区域；
4. 设计MobileViT架构融合优化后的微多普勒谱与视觉特征实现细粒度分类；
5. 基于AirSim和Wireless InSite联合生成JIVD数据集覆盖多类别/场景/天气条件。

Result: 仿真结果表明该框架可显著提升低空目标子类别识别精度，在多模态数据融合及远距离场景下表现优于单一模态方法。

Conclusion: 所提ISAC与视觉融合方案有效解决了低空目标远距离细粒度识别难题，为复杂环境下的智能监测提供了新型技术路径。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose an integrated sensing and communications (ISAC) and vision fusion framework for fine-grained low-altitude target recognition. Specifically, we first utilize ISAC system to estimate the position of the low-altitude target. Then we adjust the working parameters of the Pan-TiltZoom (PTZ) camera based on the estimated target position, such that the camera can capture the image of tiny low-altitude target from several hundred meters away. After obtaining the wireless echo signal and visual image of low-altitude target, we employ the short-time Fourier transform (STFT) to obtain the micro-Doppler (mD) spectrum of the target from wireless echo signal, and design a conditional generative adversarial network (cGAN)-based denoising network to optimize the quality of the mD spectrum. Meanwhile, we employ YOLOv11 to detect the low-altitude target from visual image, and then crop the smallsized feature image of the target from the original image. Next, we design a fine-grained low-altitude target recognition network with MobileViT, which can fuse the optimized mD spectrum and the cropped feature image to distinguish the subcategory of low-altitude target. Moreover, we generate a joint ISAC and vision dataset (JIVD) for low-altitude target monitoring based on AirSim and Wireless InSite, which includes diverse target subcategories, scenarios, and weather conditions. The effectiveness and superiority of the proposed scheme have been demonstrated by simulation results.

</details>


### [85] [On the Unilateral Approximation Condition with Linear Arrays in Near-Field NLoS Propagation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23819)
*Aniol Martí,Luca Sanguinetti,Xavier Gràcia*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文验证了近场非视距传播中大规模天线阵列单边近似的渐近有效性，为瑞利衰落模型在物理一致性的下一代无线系统中的应用提供理论依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统单边近似在视距场景中适用，但在近场非视距环境下，接收端电磁反馈至发射端的影响可能不可忽略，需重新评估其有效性以确保系统设计的物理一致性。

Method: 基于多端口通信理论，分析离散固定间距阵列与连续固定孔径阵列两种情况，考察天线数量趋向无穷时电磁互易性对信道模型的影响。

Result: 两种阵列配置下单边近似在统计平均意义上渐近成立，表明近场非视距传播中可安全忽略反向电磁耦合效应。

Conclusion: 研究结果填补了近场通信建模的理论空白，支持相关瑞利衰落模型在超大规模天线系统中的实际应用，推动6G等新技术的物理层设计优化。

Abstract: This paper investigates the validity of the unilateral approximation in near-field non-line-of-sight (NLoS) propagation for large-scale linear antenna arrays. Utilizing multiport communication theory, the study evaluates whether the electromagnetic interaction from the receiver back to the transmitter can be neglected. The analysis examines both discrete arrays with fixed spacing and continuous arrays with fixed aperture as the number of antennas increases. Results show the approximation is asymptotically satisfied on average for both cases. This provides a theoretical foundation for using correlated Rayleigh fading models in physically consistent next-generation wireless systems.

</details>


### [86] [Common-Transmitter Multipath-Aware TDoA Localization for Acoustic Backscatter-Enabled IoUT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24022)
*Ruhul Amin Khalil*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对水下物联网标签定位难点，提出基于TDoA的无时钟框架，利用接收端信号差分消除干扰因素，结合多参数加权估计实现高精度定位，实验验证性能优于现有基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有被动/半被动水下标签在弱回波与海面/海底多径效应下难以实现精准定位，传统RSSI等方法精度不足。

Method: 提出无时钟公共发射器TDoA框架，通过接收端差分消除未知询问时间、前向延迟及切换延迟，采用加权非线性最小二乘估计器融合SNR、时间方差和RMS时延扩展参数进行定位计算。

Result: 在20dB SNR条件下，相比RSSI-only等基准方法，RMSE从4.71m降至4.13m，5m范围内成功定位概率由0.793提升至0.835。

Conclusion: 本研究有效解决了水下物联网中弱信号及多径干扰下的标签定位难题，为被动式水下网络部署提供高精度定位方案。

Abstract: Acoustic backscatter enables low-power Internet of Underwater Things (IoUT) nodes, but localizing passive/semi-passive tags is difficult under weak returns and surface/seabed multipath. This letter proposes a clock-free common-transmitter time-difference-of-arrival (TDoA) framework, in which one anchor interrogates the tag and synchronized receivers measure the backscattered arrivals. Receiver-side differencing cancels the unknown interrogation time, forward anchor-to-tag delay, and tag switching delay. A weighted nonlinear least-squares estimator combines signal-to-noise ratio (SNR), timing variance, and root-mean-square (RMS) delay spread. Simulations against received signal strength indicator (RSSI)-only, unweighted, SNR-weighted, and robust TDoA baselines show that at $20$ dB SNR, the root-mean-square error (RMSE) decreases from $4.71$ m to $4.13$ m, and the $5$ m success probability improves from $0.793$ to $0.835$.

</details>


### [87] [Resource-Aware Topology Management for ISAC-Enabled TDOA Localization in IoUT Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24028)
*Ruhul Amin Khalil*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 该研究提出ISAC-TM-MAE框架，通过集成感知与通信技术和多阶段自适应估计，显著提升水下物联网的TDOA定位性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下物联网定位面临声学信道固有挑战：有限声带带宽、长传播延迟、多径效应、多普勒频移以及节点能量严重受限，导致传统定位方法难以实现高精度和可靠性。

Method: 提出ISAC-TM-MAE框架：将网络建模为图结构，在声学带宽、计算能力、能量和链路可靠性约束下选择信息丰富的节点对；采用D最优准则最大化费雪信息矩阵(FIM)；结合多阶段自适应估计(MAE)在声学噪声和信道不确定性下精炼目标位置估计。

Result: 数值实验验证，相比集中式、分布式及基准TDOA方案，ISAC-TM-MAE在定位精度、资源利用效率、网络可扩展性和复杂环境鲁棒性方面均取得显著改进。

Conclusion: 该框架有效克服了水下复杂声场限制，通过ISAC协同设计实现了高效精准的分布式定位，为水下物联网应用提供了可行的技术方案。

Abstract: Reliable localization in the Internet of Underwater Things (IoUT) is hindered by limited acoustic bandwidth, long propagation delays, multipath, Doppler shifts, and energy-constrained nodes. This letter proposes an integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled topology management and multi-stage adaptive estimation (ISAC-TM-MAE) framework for time-difference-of-arrival (TDOA) localization in IoUT networks. In the proposed framework, heterogeneous underwater sensors, seabed anchors, autonomous underwater vehicles (AUVs), and surface buoys exploit acoustic ISAC packets for joint communication and localization. The IoUT network is modeled as a graph, in which informative node pairs are selected subject to acoustic bandwidth, computation, energy, and link-reliability constraints. A D-optimal criterion maximizes the Fisher information matrix (FIM) over the underwater region of interest, while the MAE stage refines the source estimate under acoustic noise and channel uncertainty. Numerical results show that the proposed ISAC-TM-MAE improves localization accuracy, resource efficiency, scalability, and robustness compared with centralized, distributed, and benchmark TDOA localization schemes.

</details>


### [88] [On-Site Beam Calibration for RIS-Aided Positioning Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24080)
*Mengting Li,Hui Chen,Sigurd S. Petersen,Alireza Pourafzal,Huiping Huang,Ming Shen,Mikko Valkama,Henk Wymeersch*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 本文提出现场RIS波束校准框架，通过现场测量估计真实3D波束模型，显著降低定位误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度定位对智能交通等下一代通信应用至关重要，RIS技术可增强定位性能，但真实RIS波束与理想模型失配导致定位误差下限升高。

Method: 校准算法包括两步：首先用延迟域稀疏恢复从校准代理接收的信号中提取RIS反射频道响应；然后用基于梯度的估计器估计波束模型参数。通过测量66种相位调制下的3D波束模式验证。

Result: 以1度角采样，校准模型与真实波束响应的平均相似度达88.5%，理想模型仅43.7%；定位误差绝对值低于0.5m的概率从0.52提升至0.74。

Conclusion: 现场RIS波束校准有效减少由真实波束模型失配引起的定位误差下限，提升定位精度。

Abstract: High precision positioning is a key enabler for next-generation communication applications such as smart transportation and augmented reality. Reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) technology can enhance positioning by providing additional angular information and improving coverage under obstructed propagation conditions. However, true RIS beams can differ significantly from the simplified or ideal beam response models commonly used in RIS-aided positioning, leading to beam model mismatch and an elevated positioning error floor. This paper proposes an on-site RIS beam calibration framework that reduces this error floor by estimating a realistic 3D RIS beam response model from on-site measurements. The proposed calibration algorithm first extracts the RIS-reflected channel response from signals received by a calibration agent sampling the angular range of interest, using delay-domain sparse recovery, and then estimates the beam model parameters with a gradient-based estimator. To validate the proposed framework, 3D beam patterns under 66 phase modulations were measured and incorporated into simulations. With an angular sampling step of 1 deg, the calibrated model achieves an average beam response similarity of 88.5% with respect to the ground truth, compared with 43.7% for the ideal model. The probability that the absolute lower bound of the positioning error is below 0.5m increases from 0.52 without calibration to 0.74 after calibration, showing that on-site RIS beam calibration effectively reduces the positioning error floor caused by true beam model mismatch.

</details>


### [89] [Hierarchical THz Near-Field Localization with Subarray Processing and Covariance Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24149)
*Ahmad Dkhan,Yazan Dayoub,Jana El Haj,Hadi Sarieddeen*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: 针对THz近场定位中计算复杂度高和相干源鲁棒性差的问题，提出分层子阵处理框架结合Transformer协方差校正，显著降低计算量并提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: THz近场定位虽具高空间分辨率，但大阵列部署导致信号维度激增，引发计算开销；传统2D子空间算法在相干源下复杂度高且鲁棒性不足，亟需高效解决方案。

Method: 提出基于子阵（SA）处理的分层定位框架：第一步对每个子阵执行1D角度估计，第二步融合子阵输出估计距离，将2D搜索降为两个1D搜索；并设计Transformer网络预测协方差校正矩阵，优化子空间估计以应对相干源。

Result: 仿真表明算法复杂度降低四个数量级；在5dB信噪比相干场景下，角度精度提升85%，距离误差减少6.5米，显著优于MUSIC算法。

Conclusion: 该分层框架有效突破了THz近场定位的计算瓶颈与相干源限制，实现了高精度、低复杂度的实用化定位。

Abstract: Terahertz (THz)-band near-field (NF) localization offers high spatial resolution due to short wavelengths and distance-dependent wavefront curvature in NF multi-antenna systems. However, large arrays and dense deployments, necessary to mitigate THz path loss, raise the received signal dimensionality, creating computational overhead for localization. Furthermore, traditional two-dimensional (2D) subspace algorithms suffer from excessive complexity and poor robustness under coherent sources. This paper proposes a hierarchical localization framework based on subarray (SA) processing. The first step performs 1D estimation per SA to estimate local angles. The second step combines SA outputs to estimate distances, reducing the 2D search to two 1D searches. To handle the drawback of coherent sources, a transformer-based network predicts a covariance correction, refining subspace estimation. Simulations show that the proposed hierarchical algorithm lowers complexity by four orders of magnitude. The transformer-based covariance correction improves angular accuracy by 85 % and reduces range error by 6.5 m at 5 dB signal-to-noise ratio in a coherent scenario compared to multiple signal classification (MUSIC).

</details>


### [90] [ETSI ISG MAT: Bridging Multiple Access Techniques Research and 6G Standardisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24226)
*Dheeraj Raja Kumar,Mert Ozates,Yong Jin Daniel Kim,David Vargas,Onur Dizdar,Fehmi Emre Kadan,Carles Antón-Haro,Sebastian Wagner,Bruno Clerckx,Malte Schellmann,Xavier Mestre,H. M. Furqan,Kenza Abela,Shaima Abidrabbu*

Main category: eess.SP

TL;DR: ETSI ISG MAT报告通过比较多址技术，为6G标准化提供技术依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IMT-2030和3GPP 6G研究亟需提升频谱效率与缓解用户间干扰，ETSI设立ISG MAT以推动6G系统多址技术共识形成。

Method: 标准导向比较3GPP指定技术（OMA、MU-MIMO、MUST）与候选技术（功率域NOMA、RSMA、缓存辅助MU-MIMO），分析操作条件、评估收发器处理及参考信号需求；结合5G NR链路级评估与非地面网络MAT研究。

Result: 识别候选MAT提升频谱效率的适用场景，明确其对网络辅助信息和参考信号的工程影响。

Conclusion: 报告为3GPP 6G标准化提供关键技术支持，强化6G系统设计实证基础。

Abstract: Improved spectral efficiency and inter-user interference mitigation are important aspects of IMT-2030 and the ongoing 3GPP 6G study. ETSI established the Industry Specification Group on Multiple Access Techniques (ISG MAT) as a research and pre-standardisation activity to build wider consensus on downlink MAT for 3GPP-based 6G systems. Its first report, ETSI GR MAT 001 V1.1.1, provides a standards-oriented comparison of 3GPP-specified techniques, including OMA, MU-MIMO, and MUST, with candidate techniques comprising power-domain NOMA, RSMA, and cache-aided MU-MIMO. The report identifies operating conditions in which candidate MAT can improve spectral efficiency and assesses implications for transceiver processing, network assistance information, and reference-signal requirements. Ongoing work includes realistic 5G NR link-level evaluations and the study of MAT for non-terrestrial networks, providing timely technical evidence for 3GPP 6G standardisation discussions.

</details>

Abstract: Future wireless multiple-input multiple-output (MIMO) systems will integrate sub-6 GHz and millimeter wave (mmWave) bands to support high data rates for latency-critical applications. The coexistence of these bands enables the use of reliable out-of-band sub-6 GHz information to assist mmWave link establishment, particularly for channel estimation. In this paper, we propose a novel beam-domain channel estimation method for mmWave MIMO that leverages sub-6 GHz beam-domain information to improve estimation accuracy. We compare the proposed method with conventional in-band methods. Simulation results show that our proposed method outperform existing baselines in terms of spectral efficiency, in both line-of-sight (LOS) and non-line-of-sight (NLOS) scenarios.
